PS C:\WINDOWS\system32> cd "F:\tools\hashcat\hashcat-7.1.2"
PS F:\tools\hashcat\hashcat-7.1.2> #!/usr/bin/env python3
>> import json
>> import os
>> import sys
>> from collections import Counter
>>
>> def analyze_ndjson(filepath):
>>     print(f"[*] Analyzing: {filepath}")
>>
>>     attach_cycles = 0
>>     identity_requests = Counter()
>>     serving_cells = set()
>>     timing_assertion_failures = 0
>>     nas_incomplete_errors = 0
>>
>>     if not os.path.exists(filepath):
>>         print(f"[!] File not found: {filepath}")
>>         return
>>
>>     with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
>>         for line_num, line in enumerate(f, 1):
>>             line = line.strip()
>>             if not line:
>>                 continue
>>
>>             try:
>>                 data = json.loads(line)
>>             except json.JSONDecodeError:
>>                 # Handle parser gap / skipped lines gracefully
>>                 if "ServingCellTiming.num_records" in line:
>>                     timing_assertion_failures += 1
>>                 elif "NASDecodingError(Incomplete)" in line:
>>                     nas_incomplete_errors += 1
>>                 continue
>>
>>             # Check event types or message structures
>>             msg_type = data.get("type", data.get("message_type", "unknown"))
>>
>>             if "AttachRequest" in str(data) or msg_type == "attach_request":
>>                 attach_cycles += 1
>>
>>             if "IdentityRequest" in str(data) or "identity_request" in msg_type:
>>                 # Distinguish IMEISV vs IMSI type
>>                 id_type = data.get("identity_type", 3)  # Type 3 = IMEISV typically
>>                 if id_type == 3:
>>                     identity_requests["IMEISV"] += 1
>>                 else:
>>                     identity_requests["IMSI"] += 1
>>
>>             if "ServingCell" in str(data) or "cell_id" in data:
>>                 cid = data.get("cell_id", data.get("eNB", "unknown"))
>>                 serving_cells.add(str(cid))
>>
>>     print(f"    - Total Attach Cycles Detected: {attach_cycles}")
>>     print(f"    - Identity Requests Breakdown: {dict(identity_requests)}")
>>     print(f"    - Unique Serving Cells / eNBs: {list(serving_cells)}")
>>     print(f"    - Handled Timing Assertion Gaps (0 records): {timing_assertion_failures}")
>>     print(f"    - Handled Incomplete NAS Decodes: {nas_incomplete_errors}")
>>     print("-" * 50)
>>
>> def main():
>>     if len(sys.argv) < 2:
>>         print("Usage: python rayhunter_fix_and_analyze.py <path_to_ndjson_folder_or_file>")
>>         sys.exit(1)
>>
>>     target = sys.argv[1]
>>     if os.path.isdir(target):
>>         for root, _, files in os.walk(target):
>>             for file in files:
>>                 if file.endswith(".ndjson"):
>>                     analyze_ndjson(os.path.join(root, file))
>>     else:
>>         analyze_ndjson(target)
>>
>> if __name__ == "__main__":
>>     main()

PS F:\tools\hashcat\hashcat-7.1.2> #!/usr/bin/env python3
>> import os
>> import sys
>> import json
>> from collections import Counter, defaultdict
>>
>> def analyze_capture_set(base_dir):
>>     print("=" * 60)
>>     print(" RAYHUNTER CORE STREAMLINED FORENSIC ANALYZER")
>>     print("=" * 60)
>>
>>     if not os.path.exists(base_dir):
>>         print(f"[!] Target directory not found: {base_dir}")
>>         return
>>
>>     total_files = 0
>>     attach_cycles = 0
>>     detach_cycles = 0
>>     identity_types = Counter()
>>     serving_cells = defaultdict(int)
>>     parsing_gaps = Counter()
>>
>>     for root, _, files in os.walk(base_dir):
>>         for file in files:
>>             file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
>>
>>             # Process NDJSON logs
>>             if file.endswith(".ndjson") and not file.endswith("-gps.ndjson"):
>>                 total_files += 1
>>                 try:
>>                     with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
>>                         for line in f:
>>                             line = line.strip()
>>                             if not line:
>>                                 continue
>>
>>                             try:
>>                                 data = json.loads(line)
>>                             except json.JSONDecodeError:
>>                                 if "ServingCellTiming.num_records" in line:
>>                                     parsing_gaps["timing_zero_records"] += 1
>>                                 elif "NASDecodingError(Incomplete)" in line:
>>                                     parsing_gaps["nas_incomplete"] += 1
>>                                 continue
>>
>>                             # Inspect content keys safely
>>                             content_str = str(data)
>>                             if "AttachRequest" in content_str or data.get("message_type") == "attach_request":
>>                                 attach_cycles += 1
>>                             if "DetachRequest" in content_str or data.get("message_type") == "detach_request":
>>                                 detach_cycles += 1
>>
>>                             if "IdentityRequest" in content_str or "identity_request" in str(data.get("type", "")):
>>                                 # Type 3 = IMEISV, Type 1/2 = IMSI
>>                                 id_type = data.get("identity_type", 3)
>>                                 if id_type == 3:
>>                                     identity_types["IMEISV"] += 1
>>                                 else:
>>                                     identity_types["IMSI"] += 1
>>
>>                             if "cell_id" in data or "eNB" in data:
>>                                 cid = data.get("cell_id", data.get("eNB"))
>>                                 if cid:
>>                                     serving_cells[str(cid)] += 1
>>
>>     print(f"\n[+] Scanned {total_files} primary NDJSON capture logs.")
>>     print("-" * 60)
>>     print(f"• Total Attach Cycles Initiated : {attach_cycles}")
>>     print(f"• Total Detach Cycles Initiated: {detach_cycles}")
>>     print(f"• Identity Requests Breakdown  : {dict(identity_types)}")
>>     print(f"• Observed Serving Cell IDs    : {dict(serving_cells)}")
>>     print(f"• Handled Parser Gaps/Glitches : {dict(parsing_gaps)}")
>>     print("-" * 60)
>>     print("[✓] Analysis complete. No historical or inferred noise included.")
>>
>> def main():
>>     import argparse
>>     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Rayhunter Core Analyzer")
>>     parser.add_argument("--dir", required=True, help="Path to capture directory")
>>     args = parser.parse_args()
>>
>>     analyze_capture_set(args.dir)
>>
>> if __name__ == "__main__":
>>     main()
